# Plan of Record — Music-Gen campaign

**Created:** 2026-08-28T04:07:04Z
**Run id:** run-2026-08-28T040704Z

## Directive (verbatim)

Execute the Music-Gen campaign defined in music_gen_long_exposure_prompt.md at the root of this working directory (/home/user/long-exposure-runs/music-gen). The prompt's Fixed Decisions are binding. The toolchain is pre-provisioned and verified (workspace/smoke_test.py, all 14 stages green 2026-08-28). The user's rated playlists (ear bands 6/5/4) are registered with full provenance in corpus/ratings/ratings_manifest.tsv, but audio downloads are currently blocked by the workspace egress policy - see corpus/CORPUS_STATUS.md. Per the prompt, acquisition must never block downstream work: proceed on stages that do not require the rated audio, and periodically retry workspace/harvest_playlists.sh in case the network policy changes.

## Goals

| Goal ID | Goal                                                                                       | Owner       |
|---------|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-------------|
| G1      | Deliver the first end-to-end recreation spine on 5–10 songs: harvest → chunk → classify → separate → transcribe → merged score → MIDI → DAW render, with unbroken provenance. | researcher |
| G2      | Build the DAW-as-instrument layer: unattended Ardour + DawDreamer control of session, MIDI import, plugin params, automation, render — backed by a floor-and-ceiling knowledge stack. | researcher |
| G3      | Build the two judges: a hand-built heuristic battery (with intra-song meta tracker) and the trained ear (1–7 from user playlists), with non-factor leak tests. | researcher |
| G4      | Build the rules ledger and the deterministic texture layer that closes the bare-MIDI-to-original gap measured on the fixed multi-metric texture panel. | researcher |
| G5      | Generate at least one batch of new songs deterministically from the rules ledger and score them with heuristics + ear. | researcher |

| M-V3-SPINE-2/stage-checkpointed-driver | G1 | c24 operator-directed extension of M-V3-SPINE-2 per OPERATOR DECISION 2026-09-03 on Peach Dream V3_FOCUS_SONG_PARTIAL escalation. Adds per-stage content-addressed caching to the unified pipeline: each of the 9 stages writes outputs + a `stage_manifest.json` keyed on `sha256((inputs, model_weights, config, env_pins))`; on re-invocation the driver verifies the key and SKIPS completed stages. A session-budget kill costs nothing; a re-run twice still proves byte-determinism (cached stage outputs ARE the determinism evidence when keys match; `--no-cache` forces a full rerun only when the ledger requires the two-fresh-runs proof). This is within the determinism doctrine — a cached stage is a pure function of its hashed inputs. Additionally: long driver invocations should be launched detached (nohup/setsid + logfile) and polled so a session boundary never kills the computation again. Preserves c22 `scripts/v3_spine/recreate_v3.py` + `scripts/v3_spine/v3_pipeline/*` as READ-ONLY anchors by shipping the checkpointed variant as siblings (`recreate_v3_checkpointed.py`, `stage_cache.py`, `launch_detached.py`). | (a) spec doc `docs/v3_spine_stage_checkpointed_driver_spec.md` landed BEFORE any Python edit under `scripts/v3_spine/{stage_cache,recreate_v3_checkpointed,launch_detached}.py`; (b) c22 driver + pipeline SHAs byte-identical pre==post; (c) stage_cache round-trip test green (skip on match, re-run on mismatch); (d) detached-launch helper produces PID + logfile; (e) at least one stage exercised via cache-hit path. | M-V3-SPINE-2 |

## Housekeeping event pattern

Codified per-cycle housekeeping ledger-event pattern (organically emerged
since cycle 3, hardened cycle 29). At the tail of every cycle, after the
named milestone events land, the worker emits two housekeeping events:

- `_archive/cycle-<N>-scratch`: records the archival of one-shot emitters
  and investigation scratch to `tools/stale/`. `artifacts` lists each
  archived file's new path under `tools/stale/`. Prevents scratch-file
  drift and preserves the ledger's audit trail of what was written.
- `_infra/adopt-cycle<N>-tests`: records adoption of that cycle's new
  test files under the ledger. `artifacts` lists each new test path.
  Clears any `promise_check` WARNs surfaced when a test file lands
  outside a milestone-adopted directory.

Both events use `status: validated`, `confidence.level: high`, nested
`confidence: {level, rationale, assessor}`, `narrative` field (not
`summary`), pinned `run_id`, UUID5 content-hash `event_id` (auto-derived
by the ledger writer). They fire AFTER `_run/cycle_<N>_closed` — the
closed-cycle event marks the cycle's substantive milestones as landed;
the housekeeping events are pure bookkeeping and do not gate any
downstream cycle.

Rationale: consolidates the ad-hoc `_archive/<name>-scratch-<cycle>` and
`_infra/adopt-fanout-artifacts-<parent>` variants that accreted across
cycles 3–28. Naming is stable, discoverable via `grep _archive/cycle-`
and `grep _infra/adopt-cycle`, and preserves the promise-check contract
(no milestone-id drift).

## Out of scope (explicit)

- Ableton Live, Pro Tools, or any DAW requiring GUI/licensing (superseded 2026-08-28).
- Per-instrument isolation inside the "other" stem in the first milestone — deferred to a refinement round.
- Any decision, model input, or curation branch that reads a non-factor attribute.

## Pointer to ledger

Every milestone status, history, and judgment lives in `promise_ledger.jsonl`,
filtered by `milestone_id`. Run `promise_check` to materialize the current
state for the human; agents call it via Bash:

    python3 -m long_exposure.tools.promise_check .

The directive section above is **immutable** after creation. Goals and
milestones tables are mutable, but every edit must emit a ledger event with
`milestone_id: "_plan/<descriptive-change-name>"` so the audit trail is
complete.
